# SLUGS — generated index of every slug the corpus defines or cites

INDEX, not prose: do NOT read this top to bottom — opening it shows only its first rows.
GREP it, for a slug or a word inside one, with a few lines of context to catch the whole
row: `grep -C5 touches-becomes SLUGS.md`. Each row is one slug; the `##` heading carries the
defining `docID:slug` (when the slug is defined in one document) so the whole heading pastes
as a citation. Machine-generated; do not edit.

Generated by `cargo run -q -p internal-tooling --manifest-path spike/Cargo.toml -- slugs`
(the pre-commit hook regenerates it; `mise run slugs` regenerates it by hand). A row exists
while the corpus defines or cites its slug; to retire one, mark it superseded where it is
defined. Advisory `near:` lines (pointers to related discussion that does not cite the slug)
are NOT generated yet — the seam exists but no embedding backend is wired. When one lands, a
row's `near:` line refreshes only when that row's other lines change.
